text-inherit">🪄</span>Abilities System (V2)</a></li></ol></nav><h1 class="text-4xl font-bold flex items-center gap-4 grow text-pretty clear-right xs:clear-none">Triggers</h1></header><!--$--><div class="grid [&amp;&gt;*+*]:mt-5 whitespace-pre-wrap"><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">Triggers are the events that will activate an ability. Examples of triggers include a player attacking another player, a mob taking damage, they can be environmental events, or a block being broken. Triggers can also have their own parameters, such as a specific type of damage or a certain block material.</p><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">Triggers are events that activate abilities in the system. When a trigger occurs, the system checks for any abilities that are set to activate on that trigger and performs the corresponding effects.</p><div class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-width-wide:max-w-full page-api-block:ml-0 flex flex-row gap-3 justify-center"><div class="relative overflow-hidden"><picture max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug">External Triggers</div></h3><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">Here are the external triggers, which depend on other plugins or systems to work</p><div class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-screen-xl page-width-wide:max-w-full page-api-block:ml-0 table_tableWrapper__zr7LC"><div role="table" class="flex flex-col"><div role="rowgroup" class="w-full table_rowGroup__IKtSP straight-corners:rounded-none circular-corners:rounded-xl"><div role="row" class="flex w-full"><div role="columnheader" class="table_columnHeader__PGmsy text-left" decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid w-full max-w-[unset] text-start justify-self-start">CUSTOM_MOB_DEFENSE</p></div></div><div role="cell" class="table_cell__X_gFM" If you don&#x27;t wish the effect to apply immediately, you must set <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">instantApply</code> setting to <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">false</code> in the same block as effects list. That will respect the <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">time</code>
